2026-09-05T15:10:48.5291074Z #44 40.41 in a given directory, LIBDIR, you must either use libtool, and
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5291506Z #44 40.41 specify the full pathname of the library, or use the `-LLIBDIR'
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5291928Z #44 40.41 flag during linking and do at least one of the following:
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5292352Z #44 40.41    - add LIBDIR to the `LD_LIBRARY_PATH' environment variable
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5292692Z #44 40.41      during execution
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5293024Z #44 40.41    - add LIBDIR to the `LD_RUN_PATH' environment variable
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5293343Z #44 40.41      during linking
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5293657Z #44 40.41    - use the `-Wl,--rpath -Wl,LIBDIR' linker flag
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5294248Z #44 40.41    - have your system administrator add LIBDIR to `/etc/ld.so.conf'
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5294864Z #44 40.41 
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5295365Z #44 40.41 See any operating system documentation about shared libraries for
2026-09-05T15:10:48.5296034Z #44 40.41 more information, such as the ld(1) and ld.so(8) manual pages.
